SKULLPANDA continued to push the boundaries of artistic styling and              SKULLPANDA 持續突破風格與表達的邊界，與
expressive presentation and launched a co-branded series with “My                「小馬寶莉」推出聯名系列，將雙方差異化的視
Little Pony”. Such series mixes the differentiated visual styles and creative    覺風格與創作理念相融合，以繽紛色彩呈現友
concepts of both IPs, presents the infinite possibilities of friendship and      誼與創造力的無限可能，獲得全球粉絲的廣泛
creativity with vibrant colours, and has garnered widespread popularity          喜愛。「花的四幕戲」系列兼具美學張力與哲思
among global fans. The “Petals in Four Acts” series embodies both aesthetic      內涵，以花與植物為創作載體，借鑒莎士比亞
tension and philosophical depth. Utilising flowers and plants as creative        四部經典戲劇，通過花朵由萌芽、盛放至衰敗
carriers, and drawing inspiration from four classic plays by Shakespeare, this   的自然循環，呈現時間流轉、命運起伏與生命
series illustrates the passage of time, the vicissitudes of fate and the cycle   更迭，進一步豐富IP的藝術表達與敘事內涵。
of life through the natural cycle of flowers from budding and blooming to
withering, thereby further enriching the artistic expression and narrative
connotation of the IP.

driving the brand’s extension into broader lifestyle sectors.

     Non-IFRS Adjusted Net Profit                                                         非國際財務報告會計準則經調整純利
     The non-IFRS adjusted net profit has not been calculated in accordance with          非國際財務報告會計準則經調整純利未按照國
     the IFRS Accounting Standards, thus it is deemed as a non-IFRS financial             際財務報告會計準則計算，視為非國際財務報
     indicator. The non-IFRS adjusted net profit refers to the net profit after           告會計準則的財務指標，其定義為除以股份為
     excluding share-based payment expenses, while the non-IFRS adjusted net              基礎的薪酬後純利，而非國際財務報告會計準
     profit margin refers to the non-IFRS adjusted net profit divided by revenue.         則經調整純利率的定義為非國際財務報告會計
     We are of the view that such information is useful for investors to compare          準則經調整純利除以收益。我們認為，這些信
     the results of the Group, provided that the results of operation or cash flows       息對於投資者在不影響本集團經營業績或現金
     of the Group are not being affected, and enables investors to take into              流的情況下比較本集團的業績是有用的，並允
     consideration of the indicators used by the management when assessing                許投資者考慮管理層在評估本集團業績時使用
     the results of the Group. Investors shall not treat non-IFRS financial indicator     的指標。投資者不應認為非國際財務報告會計
     as an alternative or better version of the results of the Group prepared in          準則的財務指標替代或優於本集團根據國際財
     accordance with IFRS Accounting Standards. In addition, not all companies            務報告會計準則編製的業績。此外，可能不是
     will adopt the same way in calculating such non-IFRS financial indicators.           所有公司會以相同的方式計算該項非國際財務
     Hence, similar measurements made by other companies may not be                       報告會計準則的財務指標，因此未必可與其他
     comparable.                                                                          公司採用的相若計量比較。

Foreign Exchange Risk Management                                                    外匯風險管理
As the Company’s subsidiaries operate in the PRC, Asia Pacific, Americas            本公司的附屬公司在中國、亞太、美洲和歐洲
and Europe and other regions, they are exposed to foreign exchange risk             及其他地區經營，面對若干貨幣敞口（主要有
arising from certain currency exposure (mainly related to US dollar, Thai           關美元、泰銖、新加坡幣及港幣）引致的外匯風
Baht, Singapore dollar and Hong Kong dollar). Our management considers              險。由於本集團以我們經營實體各自功能貨幣
that the business is not exposed to any significant foreign exchange risk as        以外貨幣計值的金融資產及負債不重大，故管
the financial assets and liabilities of our Group denominated in currencies         理層認為業務並無任何重大外匯風險敞口。本
other than the respective functional currencies of our operating entities are       集團並沒有對沖外幣波動，但是會緊密地監控
insignificant. Although the Group does not hedge against foreign currency           有關情況，並在必要時採取措施以保證外匯風
fluctuation, we will keep a close eye on relevant developments and take             險在可控範圍內。
measures when it is necessary to ensure the foreign exchange risk is under
control.

(f) Vesting Period                                                             (f) 歸屬期
   The Board or the Scheme Administrator may from time to time while the         董事會或計劃管理人可在首次公開發售後
   Post-IPO Share Award Scheme is in force and subject to all applicable         股份獎勵計劃生效期間並在符合所有適用

   Award to be vested hereunder, provided however that the vesting               獎勵的有關歸屬標準及條件或期限，惟獎
   period for Awards shall not be less than 12 months, except that any           勵的歸屬期不得少於 12 個月，惟授予僱員
   Awards granted to an Employee Participant may be subject to a shorter         參與者的任何獎勵的歸屬期可更短，包括
   vesting period, including where:                                              以下情況：

         when leaving their previous employers;                                        獎勵或購股權；

         due to death or disability or event of force majeure;                         被終止僱傭關係的僱員參與者；


   (iii) grants of Awards which are subject to fulfillment of performance        (iii) 授出的獎勵受達成其獲授予的表現目
         targets as determined in the conditions of his/her grant;                     標所限；


   (iv) grants of Awards the timing of which is determined by                    (iv) 授出獎勵的時機由管理或合規要求釐
         administrative or compliance requirements not connected with                  定，與相關僱員參與者的表現無關，
         the performance of the relevant Employee Participant, in which                在該情況下，歸屬日期可參考獎勵若

  countries and regions. The ultimate holding company of the Company is        公司的最終控股公司為GWF Holding Limited

  4.2 Credit risk                                                                       4.2 信貸風險
      Credit risk is the risk of suffering financial loss, should any of the                信貸風險指若本集團的顧客 ╱ 客戶或
      Group’s customers/clients or counterparties fail to fulfil their                      交易對手未能履行其對本集團的合約
      contractual obligations to the Group. Credit risk of the Group arises                 責任而蒙受財務損失的風險。本集團
      mainly from exposure of trade receivables and other receivables                       的信用風險主要來自於從事經營活動
      raised by the subsidiaries engaged in operating activities.                           的附屬公司之貿易應收款項及其他應
                                                                                            收款項。


      The Group adopts the “expected credit loss” model on its                              本集團按照國際財務報告準則第9 號
      impairment assessment of debt instruments which are measured                         「金融工具」的條文，對以攤銷成本計
      at amortised cost, in accordance with the provisions of IFRS 9                        量的債務工具減值評估採用「預期信貸
      “Financial Instrument”.                                                               虧損」模型。


      As at 30 June 2026, the Group has cash and cash equivalents,                          於2026年6月30日，本集團現金及現金

      Substantially all of these bank deposits are deposited in state-                      民幣14,391,427,000 元。該等銀行存款
      owned or reputable domestic and international commercial banks                        的絕大部分均存放於國有銀行或聲譽
      which are all high-credit-quality financial institutions without                      良好的國內及國際商業銀行，該等金
      significant credit risk.                                                              融機構均為信用質量良好且並無重大
                                                                                            信貸風險的金融機構。

  4.4 Fair value estimation (Continued)                                               4.4 公平值估計（續）
      (i) Fair value hierarchy (Continued)                                                (i) 公平值層級（續）
          The Group analyses the financial instruments carried at fair                        本集團採用估值方法分析以公平值
          value by valuation method. The different levels have been                           列賬的金融工具。不同層級的定義
          defined as follow:                                                                  如下：


          Level 1: The fair value of financial instruments traded in active                   層級1：在活躍市場買賣的金融工
          markets (such as publicly traded derivatives, and equity                            具（如公開交易之衍生工具及股本
          securities) is based on quoted market prices at the end of the                      證券）之公平值乃基於報告期末的
          reporting period. The quoted market price used for financial                        市場報價。本集團持有的金融資產
          assets held by the Group is the current bid price. These                            使用的市場報價為當前買入價。該
          instruments are included in Level 1.                                                等工具列入層級1中。


          Level 2: The fair value of financial instruments that are not                       層級2：並非於活躍市場買賣的金
          traded in an active market (for example, over-the-counter                           融工具（如場外衍生工具）之公平
          derivatives) is determined using valuation techniques which                         值乃採用估值技術釐定，該等估值
          maximise the use of observable market data and rely as                              技術盡量使用可觀察市場數據而極
          little as possible on entity-specific estimates. If all significant                 少依賴於實體的特定估計。倘計算
          inputs required to fair value an instrument are observable, the                     工具之公平值所需的全部重大輸入
          instrument is included in Level 2.                                                  數據均為可觀察數據，則該工具列
                                                                                              入層級2中。


          Level 3: If one or more of the significant inputs is not based on                   層級3：如一項或多項重大輸入數
          observable market data, the instrument is included in Level 3.                      據並非根據可觀察市場數據得出，
          This is the case for investment in associates measured at fair                      則該工具列入層級3 中。按公平值
          value through profit or loss, other unlisted investment and                         計量且其變動計入損益的聯營公司
          private equity fund investments. Further details of the Group’s                     投資、其他非上市投資及私募股權
          financial assets that are measured at fair value using Level 3                      基金投資屬該等情況。有關本集團
          inputs are given in Note 4.4(ii) and Note 4.4(iii) below.                           使用層級3 輸入值以公平值計量的

5 SEGMENT AND REVENUE INFORMATION                                               5 分部及收益資料
  The Group is principally engaged in brand development, design and                本集團主要於中國及若干海外國家及地區
  sales of toys in the PRC and certain overseas countries and regions. The         從事玩具的品牌開發、設計及銷售。主要
  chief operating decision makers (“CODM”, being the executive directors           經營決策者（「主要經營決策者」，為本公司
  of the Company) reviews the Group’s internal reporting in order to               執行董事）審閱本集團的內部報告，以評估
  assess performance and allocate resources. Management has determined             表現及分配資源。管理層根據該等報告釐
  the operating segments based on these reports.                                   定經營分部。


  As at 30 June 2026 and for the period then ended, the Group had two              於2026年6月30日及截至該日止期間，本集
  reportable segments:                                                             團擁有兩個可呈報分部：


  •   The PRC operations; and                                                      •   中國業務；及


  •   The Overseas operations, which refers to countries and regions               •   海外業務（指除中國內地、港澳台以外
      other than the Chinese Mainland, Hong Kong, Macau and Taiwan.                    的國家地區）。


  The CODM assesses the performance of the operating segments mainly               主要經營決策者主要根據收入及分部業績
  based on revenue and segment results. The revenue from external                  來評估經營分部的表現。向主要經營決策
  customers reported to CODM is measured as segment revenue, which                 者呈報的外部客戶收入，作為分部收入計
  is the revenue derived from customers in each segment. Segment                   量，即各分部來自客戶的收入。分部業績
  results represent the operating profit (excluding other income and other         指各分部所賺取的經營溢利（不包括其他收
  (losses)/gains) earned by each segment, except that certain unallocated          入和其他（虧損）╱ 收益），不計及若干未分
  items are not allocated to each segment, which mainly include certain            配項目，主要包括本集團若干企業職能中
  expenses attributable to certain corporate functional centres and                心和後台職能應佔若干開支。
  backoffice functions of the Group.


  There were no separate segment assets and segment liabilities                    由於主要經營決策者並不使用分部資產及
  information provided to the CODM as CODM does not use this                       分部負債資料以分配資源予經營分部或評
  information to allocate resources to or evaluate the performance of the          估其表現，故並無向主要經營決策者單獨
  operating segments.                                                              提供此資料。

            per share is calculated by adjusting the weighted average number        薄盈利乃以假設所有具潛在攤薄影響的普通股
            of ordinary shares outstanding to assume conversion of all dilutive     獲轉換後調整發行在外普通股的加權平均數計

            assumed vested under the Share Award Scheme. A calculation is           算時乃根據股份獎勵計劃項下授予的發行在外

            acquired at fair value (determined as the average market price          價值，釐定可按公平值（按本公司普通股於報告
            of the Company’s ordinary shares during the reporting period)           期內平均市價釐定）購買的股份數目。根據上述
            based on the monetary value of the subscription rights attached         方法計得的股數與假設行使受限制股份而應已
            to the outstanding shares granted under the Share Award Scheme          發行的股數進行比較。
            (defined as the “Restricted Shares”). The number of shares calculated
            as above is compared with the number of shares that would have
            been issued assuming the exercise of the Restricted Shares.

22 SHARE-BASED COMPENSATION                                                      22 以股份為基礎的報酬
  The Company adopted the Share Award Scheme on 24 July 2020                        本公司於 2020 年 7 月 24 日採納股份獎勵計
  with a duration of 10 years commencing from the adoption date. The                劃，自採納日期起為期 10 年。股份獎勵計
  objective of the Share Award Scheme is to align the interests of selected         劃之目的乃使選定參與者的利益與本集團
  participants with those of the Group and to encourage and retain                  利益保持一致，鼓勵及挽留選定參與者為
  selected participants to make contributions to the long-term growth               本集團長遠增長及溢利作出貢獻。
  and profits of the Group.


  Under the Share Award Scheme, Pop Mart Partner Limited (the “Share                根據股份獎勵計劃，Pop Mart Partner
  Award Trust”) was established in British Virgin Islands on 7 May 2020.            Limited（「股份獎勵信託」）於2020年5月7日
  As the financial and operational policies of the Share Award Trust are            在英屬維京群島成立。由於股份獎勵信託
  governed by the Group, and the Group benefits from the Share Award                的財務及運營政策受本集團管理，且本集
  Trust’s activities, the Share Award Trust is consolidated in the Group’s          團從股份獎勵信託的活動中受益，故股份
  financial statements as a special purpose entity.                                 獎勵信託於本集團財務報表綜合入賬為特
                                                                                    殊目的實體。


  The aggregate number of RSU will not exceed 10% shares without                    在未經股東批准下，受限制股份單位數目
  shareholders’ approval subject to an annual limit of 5% of the total              合共不得超過10%的股份，並受於採納日期
  number of issued shares as at the date of adoption.                               已發行股份總數5%的年度限額規限。


  On 24 July 2020, the board of directors of the Company approved to                於2020年7月24日，本公司董事會批准以零
  issue 2,442,873 ordinary shares of the Company to the Share Award                 代價向股份獎勵信託發行2,442,873股本公司
  Trust for nil consideration in relation to Share Award Scheme. The                普通股，用於股份獎勵計劃的相關事宜。
  above issuance of shares was completed on 28 July 2020, and the RSU               上述股份發行已於 2020 年 7 月 28 日完成，
  held by the Share Award Trust was recorded as “Shares held for Share              股份獎勵信託持有的受限制股份單位列賬
  Award Scheme” and deducted from equity until the RSU are vested                   為「就股份獎勵計劃持有的股份」，並從權
  or cancelled. The number of shares under the Share Award Scheme                   益中扣除，直至受限制股份單位歸屬或註
  have been changed to 24,428,730 after the capitalisation issue on 11              銷。於2020 年12 月11 日資本化發行後，股
  December 2020.                                                                    份獎勵計劃下的股份數量已變為24,428,730
                                                                                    股。


  Pursuant to the Share Award Scheme, the Group has granted RSUs                    根據股份獎勵計劃，本集團向選定參與者
  to selected participants which include employees of the Group and                （ 包 括 本 集 團 僱 員 及 第 三 方 供 應 商 ）授 出
  third-party vendors with various vesting schedules and conditions as              受限制股份單位，並附有下文所述的各種
  described below. Once the vesting conditions of the respective RSUs               歸屬時間表及條件。各受限制股份單位的
  are met, the RSU are considered duly and validly issued to the selected           歸屬條件一經達致，受限制股份單位將被
  participants at nil consideration, and free of restrictions on transfer.          視為按零代價正式及有效發行予選定參與
                                                                                    者，且概無轉讓限制。

30 RELATED PARTY TRANSACTIONS                                                       30 關聯方交易
   Parties are considered to be related if one party has the ability, directly         若一方有能力直接或間接控制另一方，或
   or indirectly, control the other party or exercise significant influence            在作出財務及經營決策時對另一方發揮重
   over the other party in making financial and operation decisions. Parties           大影響，即視為有關聯。倘所涉各方受共
   are also considered to be related if they are subject to common control.            同控制，則亦視為有關聯。本集團的主要
   Members of key management of the Group and their close family                       管理層成員及其近親成員亦被視為關聯方。
   members are also considered as related parties.


   Save as disclosed elsewhere in this interim condensed consolidated                  除本中期簡明綜合財務資料其他章節所披
   financial information, the Group has the following significant related              露者外，本集團亦有以下重大關聯交易，
   transactions which were carried out in the ordinary course of the Group’s           該等交易乃於本集團日常業務過程中進
   business and were determined based on mutually agreed terms.                        行，並按共同商定的條款釐定。


   All the transactions with related parties were conducted in accordance              與關聯方的所有交易均按照本集團與關聯
   with the terms of contracts entered into between the Group and the                  方訂立的合約條款進行。
   related parties.
